The candidate now owns the exact authenticated original vision configuration, preprocessing values and tower tensors. Geometry and later lazy tower loading use retained verified values and the existing owned checkpoint descriptors, without reopening metadata or mapped weight paths. Request/pressure cancellation reaches the bounded tensor reads. The separate affine3-grouped-vision-memory-v1 identity retains the text-only profile unchanged and charges tower/workspace through the existing ledger.

Build v1 fails before any model because the new diagnostic omitted the required MemoryPlan.memoryLimitGB argument. The corrected build makes that fixed-plan choice explicit and gives HTTP result fields explicit local types. The resource limits and acceptance conditions do not change. Both build receipts, source inputs and the repaired source freeze are retained.

Frozen CLI 0798909be0d6b1af225d2de0f796435c97f3c09470decf2b2dbba992a709cc72 passes all 97 lightweight native reports, 49 candidate assertions and the unchanged original MTP/image diagnostic. Two real tiny images produce finite distinct feature hashes exactly matching the original tower, including a constructor whose copied metadata paths have been deleted. Corruption, pre-allocation cancellation, unauthenticated metadata fallback and impossible workspace are refused. Main-only, two-draft and genuinely reused-prefix image outputs agree; the real HTTP routes match native outputs, reject a file-URL image before expert work and remain usable for a following text request. No expert pins remain.

The respective process-tree physical peaks are 1,438,844,176, 6,952,522,960 and 9,700,955,272 bytes. Candidate and original model runs retain their separate ten- and twelve-GB ceilings, with thirteen- and fifteen-GB actual preflights and three GB minimum real headroom. The candidate's small image fixture uses a conservative fourteen-GB accounting plan without raising its ten-GB physical watchdog. This does not qualify arbitrary image sizes, image answer quality, clean timings or product Auto. The source-frozen original image/MTP gate passes without changing its existing fixture. No new weights, raw logits, installed artifacts, registry entry, tag or release are produced.

Local home prefixes are replaced with <HOME>. Original lengths and SHA-256 identify the unmodified files. Large or whitespace-bearing normalized UTF-8 transcripts are losslessly zlib-compressed and base64 encoded. Decode with `zlib.decompress(base64.b64decode(block))` and check the normalized digest. Every encoded block is verified before writing. Frozen binaries, source archives and weights remain in bounded local staging; the receipts bind their exact bytes.

### .build/quantization-research/freeze-affine-owned-vision-v1.py

Original bytes: 1212. SHA-256: `4b13cf8906d7d1a70af229109971c0c08c4258a45dc4b2e11aae2423a944a4d2`.

Normalized bytes: 1212. SHA-256: `4b13cf8906d7d1a70af229109971c0c08c4258a45dc4b2e11aae2423a944a4d2`.

````text
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,shutil,subprocess,tarfile
r=Path('.build/quantization-research');build=r/'affine-owned-vision-build-v1';out=r/'frozen-affine-owned-vision-v1'
def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
assert json.loads((build/'receipt.json').read_text())['complete']
before=(build/'inputs-before.json').read_bytes();assert before==(build/'inputs-after.json').read_bytes()
assert before==subprocess.check_output(['python3','Tools/mac_build_inputs.py',str(Path.home()/'.dbmd/bin/dbmd')])
m=json.loads(before)
assert all(sha(p)==digest for p,digest in m['files'].items())
out.mkdir()
for name in ('slotstream','slotstream-checks','mlx.metallib'):shutil.copy2(Path('.build/release')/name,out/name)
with tarfile.open(out/'sources.tar.gz','w:gz') as archive:
 for p in sorted(m['files']):archive.add(p,arcname=p)
identity={'binary_sha256':sha(out/'slotstream'),'checks_sha256':sha(out/'slotstream-checks'),'metallib_sha256':sha(out/'mlx.metallib'),'source_archive_sha256':sha(out/'sources.tar.gz'),'build_inputs':m}
(out/'build-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in identity.items() if k!='build_inputs'}))
````

````text
from pathlib import Path
import ctypes,ctypes.util,hashlib,json,os,subprocess,sys,time
sys.path.insert(0,'Tools')
from context_qualification import quiet_preflight
from prefill_bench import terminate_child_tree,vm_snapshot
r=Path('.build/quantization-research').absolute();out=r/'affine-owned-vision-v1';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
protocol=json.loads((r/'affine-owned-vision-resource-v2.json').read_text());cli=r/'frozen-affine-owned-vision-v1/slotstream';binary=sha(cli)

assert json.loads((cli.parent/'build-identity.json').read_text())['binary_sha256']==binary
record={'schema':1,'complete':False,'qualification':False,'scope':'Owned image metadata and tensors, candidate Engine/HTTP, and unchanged original MTP/vision. Two sequential model processes, no quality or timing qualification.','binary_sha256':binary,'runs':[]}
save=lambda:(out/'receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
env=dict(os.environ)
for key in list(env):
 if key.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG','VQ_','VQLAB_')):del env[key]
base='<HOME>/.slotstream/models/qwen38-flash-next-mlx-4bit'
commands=[
 ('catalogue',[str(cli.parent/'slotstream-checks'),'--tier','t0','--tier','t1','--json'],6),
 ('vision',[str(cli),'affine-vision-check','--baseline',base,'--control',str(r/'affine-expert-control-pack-v1'),
 '--table',str(r/'vq-extended-rotary-v1/rotary-output/angles-f32le.bin'),'--output',str(out/'vision')],10),
 ('original-mtp-vision',[str(cli),'mtp-check','--model',base,'--memory-gb','12','--mtp','on','--vision','on','--image',str(Path('Tools/assets/vision_test/secret1.jpg').absolute())],12),
]
allocated=sum(p.stat().st_blocks*512 for p in r.rglob('*') if p.is_file())
assert allocated+protocol['maximum_new_artifact_bytes']<protocol['maximum_research_staging_bytes']
assert __import__('shutil').disk_usage(r).free>protocol['maximum_new_artifact_bytes']+3_000_000_000
record['research_allocated_bytes_before']=allocated
record['resource_protocol_sha256']=sha(r/'affine-owned-vision-resource-v2.json')
record['original_image_sha256']=sha(Path('Tools/assets/vision_test/secret1.jpg'))
record['source_identity_sha256']=sha(cli.parent/'build-identity.json')
record['driver_sha256']=sha(__file__)
record['commands']=commands;save()

lib=ctypes.CDLL(ctypes.util.find_library('proc'));child=None;start=time.monotonic()
try:
 for name,command,limit in commands:
  before=quiet_preflight(limit+3);row={'case':name,'command':command,'before':before,'maximum_process_bytes':int(limit*1e9),'peak_tree_bytes':0,'samples':0};record['runs'].append(row);save();started=time.monotonic()
  with (out/(name+'.log')).open('w') as log:
   child=subprocess.Popen(command,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
   while child.poll() is None:
    rows=[tuple(map(int,line.split())) for line in subprocess.check_output(['ps','-axo','pid=,ppid='],text=True,timeout=5).splitlines()];pids={child.pid}
    while True:
     more=pids|{pid for pid,parent in rows if parent in pids}
     if more==pids:break
     pids=more
    total=0
    for pid in pids:
     b=ctypes.create_string_buffer(296)
     if lib.proc_pid_rusage(pid,4,b)==0:total+=max(int.from_bytes(b.raw[72:80],'little'),int.from_bytes(b.raw[240:248],'little'))
    row['peak_tree_bytes']=max(row['peak_tree_bytes'],total);row['samples']+=1
    if total>limit*1e9:raise RuntimeError('process envelope exceeded')
    if vm_snapshot()['reclaimable_bytes']<3e9:raise RuntimeError('lost three-GB real headroom')
    if subprocess.check_output(['sysctl','-n','kern.memorystatus_vm_pressure_level'],text=True,timeout=5).strip()!='1':raise RuntimeError('OS memory pressure')
    if time.monotonic()-started>1800 or time.monotonic()-start>7200:raise RuntimeError('time envelope exceeded')
    time.sleep(.25)
   row['exit_code']=child.returncode;row['seconds']=time.monotonic()-started;row['after']=vm_snapshot();save()
   if child.returncode:raise RuntimeError(name+' failed; dependent phases not run')
   child=None
  if name=='vision':
   current=json.loads((out/'vision/receipt.json').read_text())
   assert current['complete'] and current['report']['passed']
   assert current['resource_identity']=='affine3-grouped-vision-memory-v1'
   assert current['peak_process_bytes']<=10_000_000_000
  if name=='original-mtp-vision':
   text=(out/'original-mtp-vision.log').read_text()
   assert 'PASS  vision speculation deterministic' in text and 'PASS  vision speculation ran' in text

 assert sha(cli)==binary
 record['complete']=True
except BaseException as error:
 record['failure']=type(error).__name__+': '+str(error)
 if child is not None and child.poll() is None:terminate_child_tree(child)
 save();raise
finally:save()
print(json.dumps(record))
````
